Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biogeocenology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 31 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 272 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the biogeocenology space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 31 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 272. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The biogeocenology operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 873. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 946 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the biogeocenology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^873 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 946-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
